```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
```
```java
    }
}

```

```java
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;

    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure

        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

        return -1;
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
```
```java
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ loop_invariant \forall int k, l; 0 <= k < i && k < l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;
            //@ loop_invariant \forall int k; 0 <= k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ loop_invariant \forall int k, l; 0 <= k < i && k < l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;
            //@ loop_invariant \forall int k; 0 <= k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ loop_invariant \forall int k, l; 0 <= k < i && k < l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;
            //@ loop_invariant \forall int k; 0 <= k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k < i && k < l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ assignable \strictly_nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ assignable \strictly_nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;

        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k < i && k < l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
```

```java
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ assignable \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ assignable \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;

    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ assignable \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ assignable \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ assignable \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ assignable \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ assignable \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ assignable \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ assignable \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ assignable \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ assignable \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ assignable \nothing;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target;
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 0;
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```

```java
public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ requires (\max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \max(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) <= target && (\min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i]) + \min(int i; 0 <= i && i < nums.length; nums[i])) >= target;
    //@ assignable \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> (\result != null && \result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target);
    //@ ensures !(\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < j && j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> (\result != null && \result.length == 0);
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && l < n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j && j <= n;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k; 0 <= k && k < i && k < j; nums[i] + nums[k] != target;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}
```
